Mathlib.Data.Nat.Prime

Statements of Mathlib (commit a88727a581), each with its Lean source, its translation to Dedukti by Export.lean, and its informalization by Informath, in English, as a formula, and in French, Czech, German and Swedish, which the buttons at each entry show there, and the check boxes above the entries everywhere. The Dedukti translation drops implicit and instance arguments and maps Lean's arithmetic and logic to Informath's base (mapping.tsv); the other Mathlib constants are worded by the symbol table mathlib.dkgf, whose English wordings Informath also renders in the other languages. Constants without a wording appear as $\mathrm{name}$.

Mathlib.Data.Nat.Prime.Defs source

Nat.PrimedefDefs.lean:42
Nat.Prime p means that p is a prime number, that is, a natural number at least 2 whose only divisors are p and 1. The theorem Nat.prime_def witnesses this description of a prime number.
Lean
@[pp_nodot, wikidata Q49008, oeis A000040]
def Prime (p : ℕ) :=
  Irreducible p
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__Prime : (p : Elem Nat) -> Prop :=
  p => Irreducible p.
English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if $p$ is irreducible.
French
Soit $p \in \Natural$. Alors $p$ est premier si $p$ est irréductible.
Czech
Nechť $p \in \Natural$. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, jestliže $p$ je ireducibilní.
German
Sei $p \in \Natural$. Dann ist $p$ prim, wenn $p$ irreduzibl ist.
Swedish
Låt $p \in \Natural$. Då är $p$ primt, om $p$ är irreducibelt.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \operatorname{Prime} (p):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{Irreducible} (p)$

German

Swedish

Nat.Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:88
Lean
theorem Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd {p : ℕ} (pp : p.Prime) (m : ℕ) (hm : m ∣ p) :
    m = 1 ∨ m = p := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
  have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
  rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
  apply Or.imp_right _ this
  rintro rfl
  rw [hn, mul_one]
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__Prime__eq_one_or_self_of_dvd : (p : Elem Nat) -> (pp : Proof (prime p)) -> (m : Elem Nat) -> (hm : Proof (divide m p)) -> Proof (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p)).
English
Let $p , m \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime and $m$ divides $p$. Then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.
French
Soit $p , m \in \Natural$ tel que $p$ est premier et $m$ divise $p$. Alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.
Czech
Nechť $p , m \in \Natural$ takový, že $p$ je prvočíselné a $m$ dělí $p$. Tedy $m = 1$ nebo $m = p$.
German
Sei $p , m \in \Natural$ derart, dass $p$ ist prim und $m$ teilt $p$. Dann $m = 1$ oder $m = p$.
Swedish
Låt $p , m \in \Natural$ så att $p$ är primt och $m$ delar $p$. Då $m = 1$ eller $m = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural , \mathrm{prime} (p), m \in \Natural , \divides {m}{p}\vdash m = 1 \vee m = p$
Proof
6 lines
1.Let $n \in \Natural$ such that $p = m \times n$.obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
2.$m$ is a unit or $n$ is a unit.have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
3.$m = 1$ or $n = 1$.rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
4.It is enough to show that if $n = 1$, then $m = p$.apply Or.imp_right _ this
5.To prove that $m = p$, assume that $n = 1$.rintro rfl
6.$m = p$.rw [hn, mul_one]
Proof, French

3.$m = 1$ ou $n = 1$.rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
4.Il suffit de montrer que si $n = 1$, alors $m = p$.apply Or.imp_right _ this
5.Pour démontrer que $m = p$, supposons que $n = 1$.rintro rfl

Nat.prime_deftheoremDefs.lean:98
Nat.Prime p means that p is a prime number, that is, a natural number at least 2 whose only divisors are p and 1. The theorem Nat.prime_def witnesses this description of a prime number.
Lean
@[inherit_doc Nat.Prime]
theorem prime_def {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, m ∣ p → m = 1 ∨ m = p := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  have h1 := Nat.one_lt_two.trans_le h.1
  refine ⟨mt Nat.isUnit_iff.mp h1.ne', ?_⟩
  rintro a b rfl
  simp only [Nat.isUnit_iff]
  refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
  rw [← mul_right_inj' (Nat.ne_zero_of_lt h1), ← hab, ← hab, mul_one]
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__prime_def : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (and (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m => if (divide m p) (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p)))))).
English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m$ divides $p$, then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.
French
Soit $p \in \Natural$. Alors $p$ est premier si et seulement si $2 \leq p$ et pour tous les nombres naturels $m$, si $m$ divise $p$, alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.
Czech
Nechť $p \in \Natural$. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když $2 \leq p$ a pro všechna přirozená čísla $m$, jestliže $m$ dělí $p$, pak $m = 1$ nebo $m = p$.
German
Sei $p \in \Natural$. Dann ist $p$ prim, genau dann, wenn $2 \leq p$ und für alle natürliche Zahlen $m$, wenn $m$ $p$ teilt, dann $m = 1$ oder $m = p$.
Swedish
Låt $p \in \Natural$. Då är $p$ primt, om och endast om $2 \leq p$ och för alla naturliga tal $m$, om $m$ delar $p$, så $m = 1$ eller $m = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{prime} (p)\leftrightarrow 2 \leq p \wedge (\forall m \in \Natural . \divides {m}{p}\rightarrow m = 1 \vee m = p)$
Proof
9 lines
1.Assume that $2 \leq p$ and for all elements $m$ of $\Natural$, if $\divides {m}{p}$, then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
2.It is enough to show that $p$ is prime.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
3.$1 < p$.have h1 := Nat.one_lt_two.trans_le h.1
4.It is enough to show that for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $\Natural$, if $p = a \times b$, then $a$ is a unit or $b$ is a unit.refine ⟨mt Nat.isUnit_iff.mp h1.ne', ?_⟩
5.Let $a , b \in \Natural$.rintro a b rfl
6.It is enough to show that $a = 1$ or $b = 1$.simp only [Nat.isUnit_iff]
7.Assume that $a = a \times b$.refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
8.It is enough to show that $b = 1$.refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
9.$b = 1$.rw [← mul_right_inj' (Nat.ne_zero_of_lt h1), ← hab, ← hab, mul_one]
Proof, French
9 lines
1.Supposons que $2 \leq p$ et pour tous les éléments $m$ de $\Natural$, si $\divides {m}{p}$, alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩

Nat.prime_def_lttheoremDefs.lean:107
Lean
theorem prime_def_lt {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m < p, m ∣ p → m = 1 :=
  prime_def.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      forall_congr' fun _ =>
        ⟨fun h l d => (h d).resolve_right (ne_of_lt l), fun h d =>
          (le_of_dvd (le_of_succ_le p2) d).lt_or_eq_dec.imp_left fun l => h l d⟩

English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m < p$, then if $m$ divides $p$, then $m = 1$.
French

Nat.prime_def_lt'theoremDefs.lean:114

theorem prime_def_lt' {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → m < p → ¬m ∣ p :=
  prime_def_lt.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      forall_congr' fun m =>
        ⟨fun h m2 l d => not_lt_of_ge m2 ((h l d).symm ▸ by decide), fun h l d => by
          rcases m with (_ | _ | m)
          · omega
          · rfl
          · exact (h (le_add_left 2 m) l).elim d⟩

theorem prime_def_le_sqrt {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → m ≤ sqrt p → ¬m ∣ p :=
  prime_def_lt'.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      ⟨fun a m m2 l => a m m2 <| lt_of_le_of_lt l <| sqrt_lt_self p2, fun a m m2 l mdvd@⟨k, e⟩ => by
        rcases le_sqrt_of_eq_mul e with hm | hk
        · exact a m m2 hm mdvd
        · rw [mul_comm] at e
          exact a k (Nat.lt_of_mul_lt_mul_right (a := m) (by rwa [one_mul, ← e])) hk ⟨m, e⟩⟩

theorem prime_iff_not_exists_mul_eq {p : ℕ} :
    p.Prime ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ¬ ∃ m n, m < p ∧ n < p ∧ m * n = p := by
  push Not
  simp_rw [prime_def_lt, dvd_def, exists_imp]
  refine and_congr_right fun hp ↦ forall_congr' fun m ↦ (forall_congr' fun h ↦ ?_).trans forall_comm
  simp_rw [Ne, forall_comm (β := _ = _), eq_comm, imp_false, not_lt]
  refine forall₂_congr fun n hp ↦ ⟨by simp_all, fun hpn ↦ ?_⟩
  have := mul_ne_zero_iff.mp (hp ▸ show p ≠ 0 by lia)
  exact (Nat.mul_eq_right (by lia)).mp
    (hp.symm.trans (hpn.antisymm (hp ▸ Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left _ (by lia))))

Nat.prime_of_coprimetheoremDefs.lean:144

theorem prime_of_coprime (n : ℕ) (h1 : 1 < n) (h : ∀ m < n, m ≠ 0 → n.Coprime m) : Prime n := by
  refine prime_def_lt.mpr ⟨h1, fun m mlt mdvd => ?_⟩
  have hm : m ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    rw [zero_dvd_iff] at mdvd
    exact mlt.ne' mdvd
  exact (h m mlt hm).symm.eq_one_of_dvd mdvd

Let $n \in \Natural$ such that $1 < n$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m < n$, then if $m \neq 0$, then $n$ and $m$ are coprime. Then $n$ is prime.

theorem dvd_prime_two_le {p m : ℕ} (pp : Prime p) (H : 2 ≤ m) : m ∣ p ↔ m = p :=
  (dvd_prime pp).trans <| or_iff_right_of_imp <| Not.elim <| ne_of_gt H

Nat.minFacAuxdefDefs.lean:207
If n < k * k, then minFacAux n k = n, if k | n, then minFacAux n k = k. Otherwise, minFacAux n k = minFacAux n (k+2) using well-founded recursion. If n is odd and 1 < n, then minFacAux n 3 is the smallest prime factor of n.
This definition is by well-founded recursion, so rfl or decide cannot be used. One can use norm_num to prove Nat.prime n for small n.

def minFacAux (n : ℕ) : ℕ → ℕ

Swedish

theorem le_minFac {m n : ℕ} : n = 1 ∨ m ≤ minFac n ↔ ∀ p, Prime p → p ∣ n → m ≤ p :=
  ⟨fun h p pp d =>
    h.elim (by rintro rfl; cases pp.not_dvd_one d) fun h =>
      le_trans h <| minFac_le_of_dvd pp.two_le d,
    fun H => or_iff_not_imp_left.2 fun n1 => H _ (minFac_prime n1) (minFac_dvd _)⟩

Nat.minFac_le_divtheoremDefs.lean:347
Lean
theorem minFac_le_div {n : ℕ} (pos : 0 < n) (np : ¬Prime n) : minFac n ≤ n / minFac n :=
  match minFac_dvd n with
  | ⟨0, h0⟩ => absurd pos <| by rw [h0, mul_zero]; decide
  | ⟨1, h1⟩ => by
    rw [mul_one] at h1
    rw [prime_def_minFac, not_and_or, ← h1, eq_self_iff_true, _root_.not_true, _root_.or_false,
      not_le] at np
    rw [le_antisymm (le_of_lt_succ np) (succ_le_of_lt pos), minFac_one, Nat.div_one]
  | ⟨x + 2, hx⟩ => by
    conv_rhs =>
      congr
      rw [hx]
    rw [Nat.mul_div_cancel_left _ (minFac_pos _)]
    exact minFac_le_of_dvd (le_add_left 2 x) ⟨minFac n, by rwa [mul_comm]⟩

Nat.minFac_sq_le_selftheoremDefs.lean:364
The square of the smallest prime factor of a composite number n is at most n.
Lean
theorem minFac_sq_le_self {n : ℕ} (w : 0 < n) (h : ¬Prime n) : minFac n ^ 2 ≤ n :=
  have t : minFac n ≤ n / minFac n := minFac_le_div w h
  calc
    minFac n ^ 2 = minFac n * minFac n := sq (minFac n)
    _ ≤ n / minFac n * minFac n := Nat.mul_le_mul_right (minFac n) t
    _ ≤ n := div_mul_le_self n (minFac n)
Dedukti

@[simp]
theorem minFac_eq_one_iff {n : ℕ} : minFac n = 1 ↔ n = 1 := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    by_contra hn
    have := minFac_prime hn
    rw [h] at this
    exact not_prime_one this
  · rintro rfl
    simp [minFac, minFacAux]

Nat.minFac_eq_two_ifftheoremDefs.lean:383
Lean
@[simp]
theorem minFac_eq_two_iff (n : ℕ) : minFac n = 2 ↔ 2 ∣ n := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    rw [← h]
    exact minFac_dvd n
  · intro h
    have ub := minFac_le_of_dvd (le_refl 2) h
    have lb := minFac_pos n
    refine ub.eq_or_lt.resolve_right fun h' => ?_
    suffices n.minFac = 1 by simp_all
    exact (le_antisymm (Nat.succ_le_of_lt lb) (Nat.lt_succ_iff.mp h')).symm

      apply Nat.zero_lt_succ)).one_lt

Nat.coprime_of_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:406
Lean
theorem coprime_of_dvd {m n : ℕ} (H : ∀ k, Prime k → k ∣ m → ¬k ∣ n) : Coprime m n := by
  rw [coprime_iff_gcd_eq_one]
  by_contra g2
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hpdvd⟩ := exists_prime_and_dvd g2
  apply H p hp <;> apply dvd_trans hpdvd
  · exact gcd_dvd_left _ _
  · exact gcd_dvd_right _ _
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__coprime_of_dvd : (m : Elem Nat) -> (n : Elem Nat) -> (H : Proof (forall Nat (k => if (prime k) (if (divide k m) (not (divide k n)))))) -> Proof (Nat__Coprime m n).
English
Let $m , n \in \Natural$ such that for all natural numbers $k$, if $k$ is prime, then if $k$ divides $m$, then $k$ does not divide $n$. Then $m$ and $n$ are coprime.
French
Soit $m , n \in \Natural$ tel que pour tous les nombres naturels $k$, si $k$ est premier, alors si $k$ divise $m$, alors $k$ ne divise pas $n$. Alors $\NatCoprimeMACRoAN {m}{n}$.
Czech
Nechť $m , n \in \Natural$ takový, že pro všechna přirozená čísla $k$, jestliže $k$ je prvočíselné, pak jestliže $k$ dělí $m$, pak $k$ nedělí $n$. Tedy $m$ a $n$ je nesoudělné.
German
Sei $m , n \in \Natural$ derart, dass für alle natürliche Zahlen $k$, wenn $k$ prim ist, dann wenn $k$ $m$ teilt, dann teilt $k$ nicht $n$. Dann $\NatCoprimeMACRoAN {m}{n}$.
Swedish

Formula
$m , n \in \Natural , \forall k \in \Natural . \mathrm{prime} (k)\rightarrow \divides {k}{m}\rightarrow \neg (\divides {k}{n})\vdash \NatCoprimeMACRoAO {m}{n}$

6.The greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ divides $m$.exact gcd_dvd_left _ _
7.The greatest common divisor of $m$ and $n$ divides $n$.exact gcd_dvd_right _ _

Nat.Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:414
Lean
theorem Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvd {p n : ℕ} (pp : Prime p) : Coprime p n ↔ ¬p ∣ n :=
  ⟨fun co d => pp.not_dvd_one <| co.dvd_of_dvd_mul_left (by simp [d]), fun nd =>
    coprime_of_dvd fun _ m2 mp => ((prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq m2 pp).1 mp).symm ▸ nd⟩

1 lines

theorem prime_iff {p : ℕ} : p.Prime ↔ _root_.Prime p :=
  ⟨fun h => ⟨h.ne_zero, h.not_isUnit, fun _ _ => h.dvd_mul.mp⟩, Prime.irreducible⟩

def Nat__prime_iff : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (Prime p)) :=
  p => iffI (prime p) (Prime p) (h => andI (Neq p 0) (and (not (IsUnit p)) (forall Nat (a => forall Nat (b => if (divide p (times a b)) (or (divide p a) (divide p b)))))) (Nat__Prime__ne_zero p h) (andI (not (IsUnit p)) (forall Nat (a1 => forall Nat (b1 => if (divide p (times a1 b1)) (or (divide p a1) (divide p b1))))) (Irreducible__not_isUnit Nat p h) (x => forallI Nat (x1 => if (divide p (times x x1)) (or (divide p x) (divide p x1))) (x1 => iffEl (divide p (times x x1)) (or (divide p x) (divide p x1)) (Nat__Prime__dvd_mul p x x1 h))))) (Prime__irreducible Nat p).

Nat.exists_infinite_primestheoremInfinite.lean:36
Euclid's theorem on the *infinitude of primes*. Here given in the form: for every n, there exists a prime number p ≥ n.
Lean
theorem exists_infinite_primes (n : ℕ) : ∃ p, n ≤ p ∧ Prime p :=
  let p := minFac (n ! + 1)
  have f1 : n ! + 1 ≠ 1 := ne_of_gt <| succ_lt_succ <| factorial_pos _
  have pp : Prime p := minFac_prime f1
  have np : n ≤ p :=
    le_of_not_ge fun h =>
      have h₁ : p ∣ n ! := dvd_factorial (minFac_pos _) h
      have h₂ : p ∣ 1 := (Nat.dvd_add_iff_right h₁).2 (minFac_dvd _)
      pp.not_dvd_one h₂
  ⟨p, np, pp⟩

Nechť $p , k \in \Natural$ takový, že $p$ je prvočíselné a $k \neq 0$. Tedy nejmenší prvočíselný faktor $p ^ {k}$ je rovný $p$.
German
Sei $p , k \in \Natural$ derart, dass $p$ ist prim und $k \neq 0$. Dann ist der kleinste prime Faktor von $p ^ {k}$ gleich $p$.
Swedish
Låt $p , k \in \Natural$ så att $p$ är primt och $k \neq 0$. Då är den minsta prima faktorn av $p ^ {k}$ lika med $p$.
Formula
$p , k \in \Natural , \mathrm{prime} (p), k \neq 0 \vdash \NatminFacMACRoAM {(p ^ {k})}= p$
